Maturity model

Each check is tagged with a Well-Architected pillar and a maturity tier. A target's level is the lowest tier where it still has a failing or unknown error or warn check. info checks count toward scores and the roadmap but never hold a level back. Unknown results (facts postura could not read) never count as passing.

Start Missing one or more start checks.
Mature All start checks pass; missing one or more mature checks.
Advance All start and mature checks pass; working through advance checks.

About this report

Results are produced by deterministic CEL rules evaluated against settings read from the GitHub API, plus any human assessments supplied in the policy config. Automated checks cover a subset of the GitHub Well-Architected framework; pair this report with the getting-started checklist and the Well-Architected assessment for practices that cannot be verified from settings alone.
